# Skills · P · part 176

880 entries, product-discovery to product-imagery. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **product-discovery**: Use first when the product problem, target user, or desired outcome is still vague; produce an evidence-backed problem statement, assumptions, and validation plan. Do not use for fixed scope decisions or an implementable feature specification; use mvp-scope or product-spec. ([JackSmack1971/codex-skills](https://github.com/JackSmack1971/codex-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/product-discovery))
- **product-discovery**: Frame product opportunities from evidence and turn them into buildable specs: problem briefs, PRDs and job stories with acceptance criteria, prioritization, success metrics, and post-launch readouts. Use when deciding what to build, writing or editing a spec, scoring a backlog, or judging a shipped feature. Also use for squishy user problems, should-we questions, and conflicting requests. For screen design, use design-uiux; for PRD-versus-code drift, use code2flow; never change product code here. ([jamesng-minhkhoi/james-skills](https://github.com/jamesng-minhkhoi/james-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/product-discovery))
- **product-discovery**: Biến ý tưởng mơ hồ thành product brief và backlog MVP rõ ràng. ([kaivnit/skills](https://github.com/kaivnit/skills/tree/HEAD/skills/product-discovery))

- **product-experts**: Get advice from 5 product management experts from Lenny's podcast. Use when user needs PM mentorship, career advice, product strategy guidance, or help with challenges like growth, pricing, onboarding, or team management. ([Diablopop/product-experts-skill](https://github.com/Diablopop/product-experts-skill/tree/HEAD/skill) · ★7)
- **product-explainer-video**: Guided pipeline that turns a product and the problem it solves into a 30–90s explainer video through five stages — position, script, storyboard, produce, variants. Use when the user wants an explainer, product, launch, how-it-works, or onboarding video; a video script or storyboard; a rendered animated scene (HTML or MP4); or social cuts of an existing explainer. Triggers on "explainer video", "product video", "launch video", "video script", "storyboard this", "turn this into a video". ([0x4139/praxis](https://github.com/0x4139/praxis/tree/HEAD/skills/product-explainer-video) · ★4)
- **product-exploration**: Use when product, design, QA, domain, or engineering work needs a neutral navigation model from supplied evidence or an authorized live surface. ([ketzalcode/kai](https://github.com/ketzalcode/kai/tree/HEAD/incubator/kai-product/skills/product-exploration) · ★3)
- **product-exploration**: 产品探索 workflow 主技能。先进行意图判断，再面向产品经理编排竞品网页自动抓取、结构化报告生成和竞品差异面板输出。当用户提到竞品、产品立项、市场扫描、功能设计、功能迭代、动态监控、风险预警、vs、替代方案时自动触发。 ([EPetHome/Promax](https://github.com/EPetHome/Promax/tree/HEAD/docs/%E9%87%8D%E6%96%B0%E5%87%BA%E5%8F%91/02-%E9%AA%8C%E8%AF%81%E8%AE%B0%E5%BD%95/%E8%AF%81%E6%8D%AE/JUDGE-RELIABILITY-01/r11%E6%9C%AA%E5%8F%91%E5%B8%83%E6%97%A7%E7%BC%96%E8%AF%91%E5%BD%92%E6%A1%A3/promax-team-r11/2026-09-25T09-58-09-881Z/skills/product-exploration) · ★1 · 4 copies)
- **product-exploration**: Explore the reference product, analyze the PRD, and write one implementation-ready spec for a feature under docs/features/. Use when starting a new feature. ([han3zeng/terminal-portfolio](https://github.com/han3zeng/terminal-portfolio/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/product-exploration))
- **product-exploration**: Use when a new product, major capability or new market is proposed and nothing is built yet, or on /craft:product-exploration with the idea as argument. Phrases like "new idea", "should we build", "is this worth doing", "validate this idea". Ends in PURSUE, PARK or KILL. Not for features of an existing product; use `product-management`. ([heliacon/claude-skills](https://github.com/heliacon/claude-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/product-exploration))
- **product-explore**: Turn a product idea into a short, sourced decision memo that recommends build, validate first, pivot or do not build, and names the cheapest test of the idea. Use when a founder or team must decide whether, or what, to build. ([nextc/nextc-claude](https://github.com/nextc/nextc-claude/tree/HEAD/nextc-product/skills/product-explore) · ★2)
- **product-explore**: Explore a real product hands-on via computer use on a remote Mac, capturing screenshots, accessibility trees, and computed design tokens, then compile the evidence into a spec: design.md, flows, and a feature + UI/UX breakdown. Use when the user wants to reverse-engineer, document, or tear down a product (theirs, a competitor's, or a customer's target app) rather than guess at it. ([itsmaleen/agent-skills](https://github.com/itsmaleen/agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/product-explore))
- **product-explorer**: The weekly unattended run that uses the product as a persona in a local browser and files what confused it as candidate backlog items with evidence; started by the due hook on Fridays, never edits code, never touches production ([vinhto5197/hax](https://github.com/vinhto5197/hax/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/product-explorer) · ★1)
- **product-explosion**: Use when a product photo needs to become a hero "explosion" image — ingredients or parts frozen mid-burst around the product, or an exploded-view teardown with components hovering apart. Triggers on "explosion shot", "exploded view", "ingredients flying", "burst ad", "teardown render", or a request for a dramatic single-frame product hero. ([10xequity/claude-skill-library](https://github.com/10xequity/claude-skill-library/tree/HEAD/skills/product-explosion))
- **product-expressiveness**: Check a marketplace product's expressiveness and compliance evidence, including demo images, videos, cases, qualifications, and version information. Preliminary placeholder; not fully implemented. ([Websoft9/marketplace](https://github.com/Websoft9/marketplace/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/product-expressiveness) · ★1)
- **product-extraction**: Turn product documents — supplier catalogs, price lists, spec sheets, scans, phone photos of shelf labels, Excel/CSV, Word or PowerPoint, in Georgian, Russian, English or Turkish — into one checked file ready to import into a Nairos catalog (products.ndjson). Use this whenever someone wants products, prices, SKUs, barcodes or specs pulled out of a document, or wants a catalog or price list loaded, imported or added to Nairos — even if they never say "extract" or "Nairos". ([widgera/nairos-plugins](https://github.com/widgera/nairos-plugins/tree/HEAD/skills/product-extraction))
- **product-facets**: 产品多面：把一个软件项目拆成正交的「面」（功能/UI/责任/安全/发布/运维/文档…），逐面独立设计、落地、校验，反「功能能跑、其他面裸奔」的盲区，留自用→商用的升级窗口。 ([Penumbra-Noviter/useful-skills](https://github.com/Penumbra-Noviter/useful-skills/tree/HEAD/product-facets))
- **product-facilitation**: Use when any product skill or workflow needs structured intake, shared product-context state, or a single authority for next-step routing. Use on any product work that spans discovery, research, specification, or prioritization to ensure shared context and consistent routing. ([prepforeverything/prepkit-product](https://github.com/prepforeverything/prepkit-product/tree/HEAD/skills/product-facilitation) · ★2)
- **product-facing-copy-audit**: Audit user-facing product copy for leaked engineering/implementation details (paths, env flags, multi-tenant internals, debug notes). Use when editing UI toast/empty/error text, settings copy, API message/runtime_note fields shown in the client, or when the user asks to check 产品文案 / 用户可见文案 / 不要暴露实现细节. ([Sheep1433/Noesis](https://github.com/Sheep1433/Noesis/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/product-facing-copy-audit) · ★12)
- **product-factory**: Coordinate product discovery and design, then hand delivery to Make It Work.
